Hynrei kane ka miet ka long kaba dum bad kynjah. U sangot u

Ha kata ka step, ka Yew ka la dap. Lah ban shu antad ba ki 
Yadon kumba shiphew hajar ngut ki rangbah hangta bad baroh ki shu 
Ya kren siaw siaw ha ka sur kaba rit. Khatduh u Okbiwphi Esoko, u 
Yeng hapdeng uta u paid bad u pyrta sawsien, ‘‘Umophia kwenu ” 
bad katba u dang pyrta, u da phai Yalade sawdong sawdong bad i

pa kawei ka sien. Bad nangta ka sa don ka jingjarjar. U Okbiwphi 
Esoko u long u nongkren paitbah uba tbit bha bad ki ju jied Ya u 
baraborbankrenhakum kineki khep. Ujyllud iaka khlieh kaba 
lieh jong u bad syrpai Ya u tmaih lieh u jong u. Nangta u sa pynbeit 
bha Ya ka jaiti kaba u tawah na ka pohtit kamon bad kaba u teh pat 
halor ka tyipeng kadiang ka jong u. 

“Umophia kwenu ” u pyrta biang da ka sur kaba jam bad ha 
ka sien kaba san ki paitbah ruh ki pyrta jam ban jubab Ya u. Kyndit

kyndit u sei syndet la ka kti kadiang bad u kdew sh!q)hang ba don

Jong u Okbiwphi Udo, bad ha kata ka por u peit sha uta u briew uba 
shong thapiur hajan jong u, uba dem artat ka khlieh. Mar kumta hi ki

Hakatakajingialang, la don bun kiwei ruhkibalaai i'akajin^cren, 
bad ha kaba khatduh la rai ban leh kumba ju leh. La phah ai jingtip

kum ka jingsiew noh namar kata ka kam sniew kaba ki la leh.

katno ka jingrim ka jong ka. Hynrei ka la don ka jingkubur ba — ka 
jingkhlain jong kata ka dawai ka shong ha kawei ka tymmen kaba 
don tang shiliang ka kjat. Ha ka jingshisha, 'I'a kata ka dawai la khot

hamar pdeng jong ka shnong Umophia, ha i jaka ba khuid bha. Bad

¥a U Albert Chinualumogu Achebe la kha ha Nigeria ha ka 1 6 
tarik u ’Naiwieng jong ka snem 1 930 ha ka shnong Ogidi kaba don 
sh^hangmihngi jongka Onitsha, kab>ntabashaphangmihngi jong 
ka Nigeria U Achebe u heh u san bad u hynmen jong u u Rev. John 
Chukwuemeka Achebe. U pule haNekede Central School haduh 
ka kyrdan kaba hynriew. Hadien nangta u loh ban, pule medicine ha 
ka University College ha Ibadan ha ka snem 1 948 bad nangta u sa 
kylla noh la ka jin^ule jong u na ka medicine sha ka arts. U Achebe 
u la ¥oh pule i‘a ka kot ba pawnam jong ka Joyce Cary kaba kyrteng 
Mister Johnson (1939) bad dei ka jingbym sngew iahap jingmut 
jong u ’I'a ka jingthoh jong ka ba pyni i’a ka j inghiom jong ki dohlieh i'a 
kaiukom imjongki nongAphrika,dei kane kaba pynlong la u Achebe 
ba un thoh ¥a kane ka nobel ba nyngkong jong u kaba kyrteng ka 
Things Fall Apart. Kane ka nobel ka long kawei na ki nobel kaba 
pawnam jong ka spah snem kaba arphew.

U Achebe u la trei ruh kum u director jong ka External 
Broadcasting ha Nigeria ha ka por jong ka jingi'aleh thma Biafran 
bad u la ieh noh i'a kane ka kam ha u snem 1 966. 

Naduh u snem 1 972 haduh 1 976 bad biang naduh u snem 1 987 
haduh 1 988, u Achebe u la long u professor jong ka English ha ka 
University of Massachusetts, Amherst bad u la trei ruh ha University 
of Connecticut, Storrs. 

U Achebe u dei u nongthoh uba la pawnam bha bad u la thoh ruh 
'I'a kine ki kot harum : 

1. Anthills of the Savanna (1987) 

1. Arrow of God (1964) 

3. Girls at War and Other Stories (1972) 

4. A Man of the People (1966) 

5. No Longer at Ease ( 1 960)

GocIVa dei kaba u la loh award bad ka Anthills of the Savannah 
ka long kaba u la ioh la ka Booker Prize na Fingland ha u snein 
1987.

U Achebe u la ioh mh i'a ka Honorary Fellow.ship ol' the American 
Academy and Institute of Arts and Letters bad u la ioh palat arphew 
tylli ki Honorary doctorates naki University ka England, Scotland, 
United States, Canada bad Nigeria. U la pdiang nih ia ka award 
kaba heh tarn ha Nigeria na ka bynta ka jingkhraw ka jingtrei ka
